This is the combined first phase of implementing the Python composition DSL designed in `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` (recently revised — read the current version) and addressing the issues called out in `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md`. It folds together two originally-separate efforts (MP-2 typed Handles, MP-4 lifecycle/lockfile) plus a RunPod-based smoke-test scaffold so the whole thing can ship as one squashable PR.

Two later phases will build on this output: (a) MP-3 will expand the block library and add the compile-time serialization gate; (b) MP-5 will introduce VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration. Do NOT preempt their scope.

### A. Typed Handle scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

A1. `Handle` dataclass — frozen, fields: `node_id` (str), `output_slot` (str | int), `output_type` (str | None — populated later by MP-6 schema integration), `name` (str | None).
   - `__str__` returns `f"{node_id}.{output_slot}"` matching the existing string-ref convention (see `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` precedent and `vibecomfy/workflow.py:128-132` consumer that splits on `.`).
   - `__eq__` matches BOTH another Handle (by node_id+slot) AND its string form, so `handle == "12.0"` works without manual `str()`. Closes the silent type-erasure hole.
   - `__hash__` consistent with `__eq__`.

A2. Tighten `Handles` (plural wrapper at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11-35`) — change value type from `Mapping[str, Any]` to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. Update construction sites in `vibecomfy/blocks/` (notably `subgraph.py`) to wrap string refs as Handle instances. Backward-compat: callers passing raw strings must be coerced with a `DeprecationWarning` once per call site.

A3. `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs)` — typed authoring wrapper over `VibeWorkflow.add_node(...)` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122-126`). Returns an object with `.out(name_or_index) -> Handle` and `.id` (matches GraphBuilder's `id=` convention). Per the revised plan's API Concepts, this is the canonical authoring entry point. Does NOT promise mypy-grade safety; `T` parameterization deferred to a later phase that commits to overloads or codegen.

A4. `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract — implement the method signature, raise `NotImplementedError` with a clear message if `handle.output_type is None`. Documented now to prevent downstream code from forming against partial behavior before MP-6 ungates it.

A5. Lint/doctor coverage of `str(handle)` coercion — extend `vibecomfy doctor` (search `vibecomfy/cli/`) to flag:
   - Raw string refs in block code where a typed Handle is in scope (warning).
   - Sites where `str(handle)` flows into `connect()` or dict keys (warning).
   New rule name per plan §"Lint / Guardrail Ideas": `untyped_raw_ref`.

A6. Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates: build via `wf.node(...).out(...)`, assert `compile("api")` matches `compile("graphbuilder")` (modulo allowed-difference set: node IDs may move, MarkdownNote may be stripped). Use `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as one anchor.

### B. Custom-node lifecycle and lockfile enforcement (closes review §6, §7)

B1. `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` — new explicit verb. Refuses while a `run()` is in flight (see B2), closes existing `_comfy` context (mirror `stop()` at session.py:185-195 including the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow), reopens fresh `_comfy` (mirror `start()` at session.py:103-109), resets cached state carrying stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references. Implement equivalent for `ServerSession` (tear down and respawn the comfyui serve subprocess).

B2. **In-flight guard** on `session.stop()` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` — today `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (session.py:185-195) doesn't check if a `run()` is awaiting `queue_prompt_api`. Add a tracking field (e.g. `_inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None`) and have stop() either refuse with a clear `RuntimeError`, OR cancel-and-await. Audit current `session.stop()` call sites; if both options reasonable, expose `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`.

B3. **`ServerSession` external-mode handling** — when the session targets an external-managed `comfyui serve` (one VibeComfy didn't spawn), `reload_for_nodepack_change()` MUST NOT terminate it. Raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (new error class) with a clear message. Today no detection path or error class exists — both must be created.

B4. **`vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command** — write `custom_nodes.lock` after resolving any `semantic_label` values. Schema per plan §"Custom Nodes — Pinning, Hashes, and Lockfiles":
   - `git_commit_sha` (required primary pin — Git commit SHA-1, immutable).
   - `semantic_label` (optional advisory, e.g. "v1.4.0", "main").
   - `source_sha256` (optional per-node-class content digest, hex).
   Today's lockfile records `(name, sha, url)` only — see entries for `ComfyUI-KJNodes`, `ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. Extend format and migrate existing entries. Preserve `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename with backward-compat read.

B5. **`vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification verb** — read lockfile before `session.start()` (or as separate doctor invocation); verify each installed nodepack's git HEAD matches `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, verify the named class file's content hash. Default on mismatch: **FAIL CLOSED**. Provide `--allow-drift` opt-in to downgrade to warning.

B6. **Tests**: in-flight guard, reload verb (with fake nodepack), external-server detection (assert `ExternalServerRestartRequired` raised, external process untouched), lockfile round-trip, SHA verification (synthetic mismatch fails closed without `--allow-drift`, warns with it).

# Implementation Plan: P1 Foundation — Typed Handles, Custom-Node Lifecycle, RunPod Smoke Scaffold

## Overview

Goal: ship MP-2 (typed `Handle`) + MP-4 (custom-node lifecycle/lockfile enforcement) + a RunPod smoke-test scaffold as one squashable PR. Land enforcement gates from the DSL plan and review without preempting MP-3 (block library / serialization gate), MP-5 (VibeFlow), or MP-6 (schema-backed `output_type` / `run_until` ungating).

Repo shape that matters:
- `Handles` (plural) exists at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11`; values are `Mapping[str, Any]`. `Handle` (singular) does **not** exist.
- String-ref construction precedent at `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` (`f"{node.id}.{slot}"`); other block construction sites in `blocks/loaders.py`, `blocks/encoding.py`, `blocks/sampling.py`, `blocks/save.py`, `blocks/latent.py` return bare-node-id strings (e.g. `Handles({"image": "1"})` per `tests/test_blocks.py:41`).
- `VibeWorkflow.add_node` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122`) returns a mutable `VibeNode` with no typed-output wrapper. `connect` (`:128`) parses `from_ref.split(".", 1)`. `compile("api")` (`:176`) doesn't accept `Handle`s in inputs.
- `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:185`) has no in-flight guard. `queue_prompt_api` await at `:144` is unshielded. `ServerSession.reconfigure` (`:281`) only reacts to argv changes.
- `ServerSession` distinguishes external vs. owned server only by `self.process is None` (`:202`). No `ExternalServerRestartRequired` exists.
- `custom_nodes.lock` is a 2-line `<name> <git_sha> <url>` file (no reader, no verifier).
- `vibecomfy nodes` command is at `vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py` (subcommands `list`, `spec`, `install-plan`); `vibecomfy doctor` is at `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`. There is no `vibecomfy/cli/` directory — all command modules live under `vibecomfy/commands/`. `cli/main` lives at `vibecomfy/cli.py`.
- `runpod_lifecycle` is a sibling repo loaded via `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT` or `Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "runpod-lifecycle"` (see `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py:10-17`).
- pytest config is minimal (`pyproject.toml:33-34`): `asyncio_mode = "auto"`, no markers registered.
- Parity reference: `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` (uses private `node()` helper at `:172`, raw `workflow.connect` strings — proves native builders work but is **not** the target API shape per plan §"Reconciliation").

Constraints (hard):
- Backward compatibility: every existing test passes unchanged (one calibration: `tests/test_blocks.py` asserts `handles.image == "1"` — `Handle.__eq__` must accept the bare-node-id form when `output_slot == 0` so this keeps holding).
- Old lockfile schema (`name sha url`) loads via migration shim.
- No mypy-grade typing claim (P1 = typed metadata + lint, per review §2).
- No serialization gate (MP-3), no schema/`output_type` integration (MP-6), no VibeFlow (MP-5), no `wrap_api_dict` (MP-5 territory).
- Smoke test is **scaffolded only**, never executed by the megaplan run.

## Phase A: Typed Handle Scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

### Step 1: Add `Handle` dataclass and tighten `Handles` (`vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** new frozen dataclass `Handle` with fields `node_id: str`, `output_slot: str | int = 0`, `output_type: str | None = None`, `name: str | None = None`. Implement:
   - `__str__` → `f"{self.node_id}.{self.output_slot}"`.
   - `__eq__(other)` accepts another `Handle` (compares `node_id` + `output_slot`), the canonical string `f"{node_id}.{output_slot}"`, and — for backward compat — the bare `node_id` string when `output_slot == 0`. Falls through to `NotImplemented` otherwise.
   - `__hash__` returns `hash((self.node_id, str(self.output_slot)))` so it is consistent with `__eq__` against the canonical string form.
2. **Tighten** `Handles.values` typing to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. In `Handles.__init__` coerce raw strings: parse `"node.slot"` into `Handle(node_id, output_slot)` (split on first `.`); a bare `"id"` becomes `Handle(id, 0)`. Each coercion emits `warnings.warn(..., DeprecationWarning, stacklevel=2)` exactly once per source location (use a module-level `set` keyed on `(filename, lineno)` from `inspect.stack()[1]`).
3. **Re-export** `Handle` from `vibecomfy.blocks` and from `vibecomfy` top-level (`vibecomfy/__init__.py`) so users get a clean public path: `from vibecomfy import Handle`.

### Step 2: Wrap string refs at construction sites (`vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py`, `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py`, plus `loaders.py`, `encoding.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `latent.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` to construct `Handle` objects directly:
   ```python
   return Handles(
       {name: Handle(node_id=node.id, output_slot=slot, name=name) for slot, name in enumerate(outputs)},
       node=Handle(node_id=node.id, output_slot=0),
   )
   ```
2. **Audit** the other block modules (`loaders.py`, `encoding.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `latent.py`) for `Handles({"x": "<id>"})` and `Handles({"x": f"{node.id}.{slot}"})` patterns. Replace with `Handle(...)`. Where `Handles({"image": "1"})` was returning a bare node id, the equivalent is `Handle("1", 0, name="image")` — `__eq__` keeps the existing test assertions valid.
3. **Preserve** the `node` provenance entry pattern (the kwarg in `Handles({...}, node=node.id)`) by making it a `Handle` too.

### Step 3: Add `wf.node(...)` typed authoring wrapper (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** an internal class `_NodeBuilder` (or `NodeRef`) returned by a new public method `VibeWorkflow.node(self, class_type: str, **kwargs) -> _NodeBuilder`:
   ```python
   def node(self, class_type: str, **kwargs: Any) -> "_NodeBuilder":
       node = self.add_node(class_type)
       for key, value in kwargs.items():
           if isinstance(value, Handle):
               self.connect(str(value), f"{node.id}.{key}")
           else:
               # widget/literal - mirror VibeNode shape: literals -> inputs
               node.inputs[key] = value
       return _NodeBuilder(workflow=self, node=node)
   ```
   `_NodeBuilder` exposes:
   - `id: str` (mirrors GraphBuilder's `id=` convention).
   - `out(name_or_index: str | int) -> Handle` returning `Handle(node_id=self.node.id, output_slot=name_or_index, name=name_or_index if isinstance(name_or_index, str) else None)`. `output_type` stays `None` — MP-6 populates it.
2. **Extend** `VibeWorkflow.connect` to accept `Handle` arguments without breaking the string path. New signature: `connect(self, from_ref: str | Handle, to_ref: str | Handle) -> "VibeWorkflow"`. Coerce `Handle` via `str(handle)` (which produces the canonical `node.slot` form) before splitting.
3. **Defer** `T` parameterization — `Handle` is intentionally non-generic for P1 (review §2).

### Step 4: Implement `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `def run_until(self, handle: Handle) -> Any:` that raises `NotImplementedError(...)` with this exact contract message: `"wf.run_until is gated on MP-6 schema integration; handle.output_type is None. Manually attach SaveImage/PreviewImage/SaveAudio."` Refuse only when `handle.output_type is None`. If a future caller hand-populates `output_type`, fall through to a second `NotImplementedError(...)` saying the runner itself is not implemented yet (so neither path forms against partial behavior).

### Step 5: Doctor lint rule `untyped_raw_ref` (`vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** an AST-walker pass for Python builder paths (`.py` workflow refs only — JSON refs skip). Use `ast.parse` on the source file resolved by `_json_path_for_reference`'s sibling logic, or load via `inspect.getsource(build_module)` after the `load_workflow_reference` import.
2. **Flag** two patterns as `untyped_raw_ref` warnings (do not error):
   - **Source side:** a string literal of shape `r"^\w+\.\w+$"` passed positionally to `workflow.connect(...)` when a `Handle`-returning call is in scope (heuristic: any call to `wf.node(...).out(...)` earlier in the same function).
   - **Coercion side:** a call expression `str(<name>)` where `<name>` is bound to a `Handle` (single-assignment heuristic; only flag when the binding is `... .out(...)`).
3. **Emit** warnings via stdout in the same shape as existing doctor output (prefixed with `- untyped_raw_ref:`) and exit code 0 (warning only). Behind `args.lint` flag (`--lint`) — default off so existing doctor invocations are unchanged.

### Step 6: Parity tests (`tests/test_ready_templates.py` or new `tests/test_handle_parity.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** three small builder fixtures using `wf.node(...).out(...)` exclusively. One mirrors `flux2_klein_4b_t2i` semantics (UNETLoader → CLIPTextEncode → KSampler → VAEDecode → SaveImage, `MarkdownNote` excluded from parity diff). The other two cover (a) a single opaque/UUID subgraph node (mirrors the Flux Klein subgraph case) and (b) a multi-output node feeding two consumers.
2. **Assert** `wf.compile("api") == wf.compile("graphbuilder")` modulo the allowed-difference set (skip `MarkdownNote` nodes if any, normalize node-ID ordering before compare). Mark the test `@pytest.mark.skipif(importlib.util.find_spec("comfy_execution") is None, ...)` like the existing `test_graphbuilder_backend_matches_api_backend` at `tests/test_workflow_core.py:67`.
3. **Add** a non-skipped test that asserts `wf.compile("api")` shape (class types, edge wiring) for those three fixtures so the parity claim has a fallback when GraphBuilder isn't installed.

### Step 7: Handle/wrapper unit tests (`tests/test_handle.py` — new)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cover:** `Handle.__str__`, `__eq__` against another Handle, against `"node.slot"`, against bare `"node"` (slot=0 only), `__hash__` consistency, `Handles` coercion-with-DeprecationWarning (use `pytest.warns`), top-level `from vibecomfy import Handle` import path, `wf.node(...).out(name)` returning a Handle that compiles cleanly through `compile("api")`, `wf.run_until(handle)` raising `NotImplementedError`.

## Phase B: Custom-Node Lifecycle and Lockfile (closes review §6, §7)

### Step 8: In-flight run tracking on sessions (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `self._inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None = None` to both `EmbeddedSession.__init__` and `ServerSession.__init__`.
2. **In** `EmbeddedSession.run` and `ServerSession.run` wrap the `queue_prompt_api`/HTTP queue call: set `self._inflight_run = asyncio.shield(asyncio.ensure_future(...))` before `await`, clear it in `finally`.
3. **Modify** `EmbeddedSession.stop` and `ServerSession.stop` to take `wait_for_inflight: bool = True` parameter:
   - If `_inflight_run` is set and not done and `wait_for_inflight is True`: await it (don't cancel — HiddenSwitch can't be aborted mid-prompt per SD-013(a)).
   - If `_inflight_run` is set and `wait_for_inflight is False`: raise `RuntimeError("session.stop() called while a run is in flight; pass wait_for_inflight=True or call after run completes")`.
   - Existing behavior (no in-flight) is unchanged so all callers in `tests/test_runtime_session.py` still pass.

### Step 9: `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `async def reload_for_nodepack_change(self, *, reason: str) -> None` to `EmbeddedSession`. Logic:
   - If `_inflight_run` is set and not done: raise `RuntimeError("reload refused: run in flight")`.
   - Mirror `stop()`'s `_context.__aexit__` block (preserve the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow at `:190-192`).
   - Reset `self._comfy = None`, `self._context = None`, `self._schema_provider = None`, `self._schema_warning_emitted = False`, `self.last_fingerprint = None` (clear cached state that may carry stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS`).
   - Call `await self.start()` to reopen a fresh `Comfy()` context.
   - Log: `logger.info("reload_for_nodepack_change: %s", reason)`.
2. **Add** equivalent method on `ServerSession`:
   - If `self.process is None`: raise new `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (defined in same module) with message `f"vibecomfy does not own this server (url={self.url}); restart externally and reconnect to apply nodepack change ({reason})"`. Do **not** kill the process.
   - If `self.process is not None`: respect in-flight guard, then `await self.stop()` followed by `await self.start()`.

### Step 10: `ExternalServerRestartRequired` exception (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Define** `class ExternalServerRestartRequired(RuntimeError): ...` near the top of `session.py` (with docstring referencing the lifecycle plan section). Export it from `vibecomfy.runtime` (update `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` if it currently re-exports session symbols).

### Step 11: Lockfile schema migration + reader/writer (`vibecomfy/node_packs/lockfile.py` — new module)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/node_packs/lockfile.py` with:
   - Dataclass `LockEntry(name: str, git_commit_sha: str, url: str, semantic_label: str | None = None, source_sha256: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict))` where `source_sha256` maps class-file relative path → hex digest.
   - `def read_lockfile(path: Path = Path("custom_nodes.lock")) -> list[LockEntry]`. Auto-detect format:
     - Legacy line: 3 whitespace-separated tokens → `LockEntry(name, git_commit_sha=token2, url=token3)`. Accept `git_sha` and `git_commit_sha` as synonyms when reading the structured form.
     - New format: TOML or extended whitespace per the schema below — choose extended whitespace with optional `key=value` extensions on continuation lines, OR migrate to TOML keyed by name. **Decision:** use TOML (`[ComfyUI-KJNodes]` style) for the new format; keep legacy single-line readable as a fallback. Rationale: extending whitespace cleanly to dict-valued `source_sha256` is awkward; TOML is in stdlib (`tomllib`).
   - `def write_lockfile(entries: list[LockEntry], path: Path) -> None` — always writes TOML. Migrates legacy entries on the next `vibecomfy nodes lock` invocation.

### Step 12: `vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command (`vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Register** new subcommand `lock` with `_cmd_nodes_lock(args)`:
   - Reads existing lockfile (legacy or new) via `read_lockfile`.
   - For each entry, resolves `semantic_label` to a `git_commit_sha` if a checkout is present (run `git -C <pack_dir> rev-parse HEAD`). If no checkout: keep existing `git_commit_sha`, leave `semantic_label` advisory.
   - Optional `--with-source-sha256` flag (default off) computes per-class file digests for installed packs by walking `<pack_dir>` and hashing `*.py` files with `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references.
   - Writes new TOML lockfile.
2. **Update** `register(subparsers)` block at `vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py:101` to add the new subparser.

### Step 13: `vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification (`vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** verification pass: read lockfile via `read_lockfile`. For each entry, locate the installed nodepack (search known paths: `vendor/<name>`, `custom_nodes/<name>`, or wherever `resolve_node_packs()` consumers look — inspect `vibecomfy/node_packs/` to confirm the canonical install root). Run `git -C <pack_dir> rev-parse HEAD` and compare to `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` is present, hash the corresponding files and compare.
2. **Mismatch policy:** default fail-closed (return `1` with `Layer: nodepack lockfile drift` output). Add `--allow-drift` flag that downgrades drift to warnings (return `0`).
3. **Wire** into `_cmd_doctor` *before* the existing schema validation pass so a mismatched pack short-circuits with a clear error before downstream confusion.
4. **Also expose** as a standalone subcommand `vibecomfy doctor lockfile <workflow_path>` if the CLI dispatcher pattern allows; otherwise just as the integrated step.

### Step 14: Lifecycle, lockfile, and doctor tests (`tests/test_runtime_session.py`, `tests/test_nodes_lock.py` — new, `tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **In-flight guard** (extend `tests/test_runtime_session.py`): use the existing `FakeComfy` fixture; arrange a `queue_prompt_api` that blocks on an `asyncio.Event`; call `stop(wait_for_inflight=False)` and assert `RuntimeError`; with `wait_for_inflight=True`, set the event and assert clean teardown.
2. **Reload verb (embedded):** assert `reload_for_nodepack_change(reason="test")` calls `__aexit__` then `__aenter__` once each, that `_comfy` is a fresh `FakeComfy` instance, and the in-flight guard refusal triggers when a run is pending.
3. **External-server detection:** spin a `ServerSession` whose `process is None` (assigned manually after instantiation, simulating reconnect-to-external mode). Assert `reload_for_nodepack_change(reason=...)` raises `ExternalServerRestartRequired` and does **not** mutate `self.process`/`self.url`.
4. **Lockfile round-trip** (`tests/test_nodes_lock.py`): write a legacy file, read it, write back as TOML, read TOML, confirm parity. Confirm `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename works on read.
5. **SHA verification** (`tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py`): create a fake pack dir with a fake `.git/HEAD` (or monkeypatch the `git rev-parse` subprocess call). Test (a) match → success; (b) mismatch without `--allow-drift` → exit 1; (c) mismatch with `--allow-drift` → warning, exit 0.

## Phase C: RunPod Smoke-Test Scaffold

### Step 15: Register `runpod` pytest marker (`pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** under `[tool.pytest.ini_options]`:
   ```toml
   markers = [
     "runpod: opt-in smoke tests that provision real RunPod GPU pods (requires RUNPOD_API_KEY; not run by default)",
   ]
   ```

### Step 16: Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `tests/smoke/__init__.py` (empty) and `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py`.
2. **Test body**:
   - At module top: `pytestmark = pytest.mark.runpod`.
   - Skip if `os.environ.get("RUNPOD_API_KEY")` is unset (graceful skip on opt-in invocations without creds).
   - Import `runpod_lifecycle` (handle `ImportError` → `pytest.skip` referencing `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT` per `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py:10-17`).
   - Provision one cheap pod (RTX 4090 or A40) using `runpod_lifecycle`'s provisioning API. Use a `try/finally` so teardown runs on success **and** failure.
   - On the pod: install vibecomfy from current branch (rsync or `pip install git+...`), import, build a 3-node workflow via `wf.node(...).out(...)` (CLIPTextEncode → KSampler stub → SaveImage, or the Phase A parity fixture), call `await session.reload_for_nodepack_change(reason="smoke")`, then `await session.run(wf)`.
   - Assert at least one output path exists in `result.outputs`.
   - Tear down the pod in `finally`.
3. **Do not call this test in this megaplan run.** No `pytest -m runpod` invocation, no provisioning. Acceptance verifies file existence + marker + imports.

### Step 17: Document the smoke contract (`docs/runpod_smoke.md` — new, plus pointer in `docs/custom_nodes.md` or `tests/README.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Document**: how to run (`pytest -m runpod tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py`), required env (`RUNPOD_API_KEY`, `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT`), expected wall-clock + cost (one cheap-GPU pod for ~5–10 min ≈ $0.05–$0.20), and the safety contract: teardown runs on success and failure; the test never runs in default `pytest` invocations because the marker is unrecognized in default config (configure `[tool.pytest.ini_options]` `addopts` to NOT auto-include `-m runpod`).

## Validation Order

1. Run `pytest tests/test_handle.py` (Phase A unit tests — fastest signal).
2. Run `pytest tests/test_blocks.py tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_ready_templates.py` (catches Handle migration regressions).
3. Run `pytest tests/test_runtime_session.py tests/test_nodes_lock.py tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py` (Phase B).
4. Run `pytest` (full suite, no markers) — verifies the runpod test is correctly excluded by default.
5. Verify (do not execute) the smoke test: `pytest --collect-only -m runpod` should list exactly the new file.
6. Sanity-check `vibecomfy doctor <ready_template>` with the synthetic drift scenario manually if time allows.

## Execution Order

1. Phase A first (Handle + wrapper + parity tests + lint). This unblocks the Phase B reload tests, which benefit from typed handles in their fixtures, and prevents accidental coercion regressions during Phase B refactors.
2. Phase B second (lifecycle + lockfile + doctor). Handle types are stable by then; lifecycle changes only touch session.py + commands/nodes.py + commands/doctor.py.
3. Phase C last (smoke scaffold). Pure additive — only depends on the fact that `wf.node(...).out(...)` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` exist, which are delivered by Phases A+B.

{"checks":[{"id":"issue_hints","question":"Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?","findings":[{"detail":"Cross-checked deliverable A6 against the plan's Phase A Step 6. The brief asks for parity tests for at least three ready templates, with `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as an anchor, but the plan describes adding three small synthetic builder fixtures that only mirror ready-template semantics. That is a scope divergence: synthetic fixtures can prove `wf.node(...).out(...)` compiles, but they do not prove parity against the actual ready-template corpus requested by acceptance.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked C1/C2 against Phase C. The plan marks the smoke test with `@pytest.mark.runpod` and skips when `RUNPOD_API_KEY` is unset, but it does not make the test opt-in when credentials are present. In pytest, registering a marker does not exclude collection or execution, so a default `pytest` run in an environment with `RUNPOD_API_KEY` could provision a real pod, contradicting the brief's 'never runs in default pytest invocations or CI without explicit opt-in' requirement.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked B3 against the current `ServerSession` shape in `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`. The plan uses `self.process is None` as external-server detection, but a newly constructed or stopped managed `ServerSession` also has `process is None`; the brief explicitly says no detection path exists and must be created. The plan therefore risks classifying an owned-but-stopped managed session as external instead of tracking ownership mode explicitly.","flagged":true}]},{"id":"correctness","question":"Are the proposed changes technically correct?","findings":[{"detail":"Checked the proposed `.out(name_or_index)` behavior against `VibeWorkflow.compile` in `vibecomfy/workflow.py`. The plan returns `Handle(node_id, output_slot=name_or_index)` for string names such as `'CONDITIONING'` and routes that through `connect(str(handle), ...)`, but `compile('api')` converts every edge output with `int(edge.from_output)`. A workflow using the desired authoring style `wf.node(...).out('CONDITIONING')` will raise `ValueError` at compile time unless P1 adds a name-to-slot mapping or restricts named outputs to numeric strings.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked the import graph around the proposed `Handle` location. `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` currently imports `VibeWorkflow` from `vibecomfy.workflow`, while the plan puts `Handle` in `vibecomfy.blocks` and needs `workflow.py` to import it for `isinstance(value, Handle)` in `VibeWorkflow.node` and `connect`. A top-level import in that direction creates a circular import risk; the plan should put `Handle` in a neutral module or specify lazy imports.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked the planned `connect(self, from_ref: str | Handle, to_ref: str | Handle)` signature against the meaning of `Handle`. A `Handle` identifies an output `(node_id, output_slot)`, while `to_ref` is a target input path `(node_id, input_name)`. Accepting a `Handle` as `to_ref` would produce targets such as `node.0` instead of named inputs and is not supported by the current IR semantics.","flagged":true}]},{"id":"scope","question":"Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?","findings":[{"detail":"Searched for `Handles(` across `vibecomfy/blocks/`. The plan names `subgraph.py`, `loaders.py`, `encoding.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, and `latent.py`, but the current code also returns raw node-id strings in `vibecomfy/blocks/decode.py` and `vibecomfy/blocks/video.py`. The plan's 'audit other block modules' wording may cover them, but the explicit migration list is incomplete and should call out those modules to avoid leaving mixed Handle/string returns.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked related authoring documentation in `docs/authoring.md`. It currently demonstrates blocks returning `Handles(text=node.id)` and describes the old string-ref contract. The plan updates RunPod docs and custom-node docs, but does not include a doc update for the primary block-authoring page, so users would still be taught the deprecated raw-string `Handles` style immediately after P1 lands.","flagged":true}]},{"id":"all_locations","question":"Does the change touch all locations AND supporting infrastructure?","findings":[{"detail":"Checked pytest configuration in `pyproject.toml`. Adding a marker entry alone is not supporting infrastructure for opt-in smoke tests; pytest will still collect and run marked tests unless they self-skip or the default selection excludes them. The plan needs an explicit gate such as `pytest_collection_modifyitems` requiring `--runpod`, or a skip condition that checks `-m runpod` was explicitly requested, not just whether credentials exist.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked runtime package exports in `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py`. The plan explicitly includes exporting `ExternalServerRestartRequired`, but it does not mention exporting `EmbeddedSession` or `ServerSession` themselves even though acceptance says `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` is exposed in `vibecomfy.runtime`. Today `vibecomfy.runtime` exports run helpers only, so exposing the new lifecycle verb through the package requires a broader `__init__.py` update than the plan states.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked command registration structure in `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`. The plan proposes `vibecomfy doctor --lint <path>` and possibly `vibecomfy doctor lockfile <workflow_path>`, but `doctor` is currently a single parser with one positional `path`, not a subcommand group. This is implementable, but the plan does not spell out the parser migration needed to add `--lint`, `--allow-drift`, and a potential `lockfile` subcommand without breaking existing `vibecomfy doctor PATH` invocations.","flagged":true}]},{"id":"callers","question":"Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?","findings":[{"detail":"Checked callers of `workflow.connect` and the block helper `vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect`. Existing block callers pass bare node ids, dotted strings, and `VibeNode` instances; after the plan migrates block return values to `Handle`, those handles will commonly flow through `_utils.connect`, which currently accepts only `str | VibeNode | None`. The plan updates `VibeWorkflow.connect`, but it must also update `_utils.node_id`/`connect` typing and behavior deliberately or the main block-helper caller path remains an implicit `str(handle)` coercion path.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked callers of `ServerSession.stop` in tests and command code. Existing code calls `await session.stop()` with no arguments, so adding `wait_for_inflight: bool = True` is backwards compatible at the call-site level. The risk is semantic rather than syntactic: if stop waits only for the queued prompt future, as the plan says, it may close the process/context before the rest of `run()` finishes watchdog finalization and metadata writes.","flagged":true},{"detail":"Checked direct `VibeWorkflow.connect` test callers in `tests/test_edge_primitives.py`, `tests/test_workflow_core.py`, and patches. They pass string refs like `'A.0'`, `'B.in'`, and formatted raw strings, so preserving the string path is necessary and the plan does preserve it. No additional positional or `None` caller shape was found for direct `connect`.","flagged":false}]}],"flags":[{"id":"FLAG-001","concern":"Ready-template parity: plan substitutes synthetic `wf.node(...).out(...)` fixtures for the requested parity tests against at least three actual ready templates, so acceptance A6 would not be proven against the corpus.","category":"completeness","severity_hint":"likely-significant","evidence":"Plan Phase A Step 6 says 'Add three small builder fixtures' and 'mirrors `flux2_klein_4b_t2i` semantics'; the brief says 'Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates' with `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as an anchor."},{"id":"FLAG-002","concern":"Named output handles: `.out('CONDITIONING')` or other nonnumeric names will compile into an edge output that `compile('api')` casts with `int(edge.from_output)`, causing `ValueError`.","category":"correctness","severity_hint":"likely-significant","evidence":"`vibecomfy/workflow.py` stores `VibeEdge.from_output` as a string and `compile` emits `[str(edge.from_node), int(edge.from_output)]`; plan Step 3 returns `Handle(..., output_slot=name_or_index)` for arbitrary string names."},{"id":"FLAG-003","concern":"RunPod opt-in: marker registration plus credential-based skip is insufficient; default `pytest` can execute the real RunPod smoke test whenever credentials are present.","category":"correctness","severity_hint":"likely-significant","evidence":"`pyproject.toml` currently has no marker filtering or collection hook. Plan Step 15 only registers the marker, and Step 16 skips on missing `RUNPOD_API_KEY`; pytest does not exclude marked tests by default."},{"id":"FLAG-004","concern":"Import architecture: placing `Handle` in `vibecomfy.blocks` while `workflow.py` needs to import it for `wf.node`/`connect` creates a circular dependency because `vibecomfy.blocks.__init__` already imports `VibeWorkflow`.","category":"maintainability","severity_hint":"likely-significant","evidence":"Current `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` imports `from vibecomfy.workflow import VibeWorkflow`; plan Step 1 defines `Handle` there and plan Step 3 needs `workflow.py` to check `isinstance(value, Handle)`."},{"id":"FLAG-005","concern":"ServerSession ownership detection: `process is None` is ambiguous between external server mode and managed session not yet started or already stopped, so `reload_for_nodepack_change` can raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` for a session VibeComfy actually owns.","category":"correctness","severity_hint":"likely-significant","evidence":"Current `ServerSession.__init__` initializes `process = None` and `url = None`; plan Step 9 says `if self.process is None: raise ExternalServerRestartRequired` without adding an explicit ownership/external-mode field."}],"verified_flag_ids":[],"disputed_flag_ids":[]}

        Idea:
P1 Foundation: typed Handle scaffold + custom-node lifecycle/lockfile + RunPod smoke-test scaffold.

This is the combined first phase of implementing the Python composition DSL designed in `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` (recently revised — read the current version) and addressing the issues called out in `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md`. It folds together two originally-separate efforts (MP-2 typed Handles, MP-4 lifecycle/lockfile) plus a RunPod-based smoke-test scaffold so the whole thing can ship as one squashable PR.

Two later phases will build on this output: (a) MP-3 will expand the block library and add the compile-time serialization gate; (b) MP-5 will introduce VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration. Do NOT preempt their scope.

DELIVERABLES — all must land:

### A. Typed Handle scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

A1. `Handle` dataclass — frozen, fields: `node_id` (str), `output_slot` (str | int), `output_type` (str | None — populated later by MP-6 schema integration), `name` (str | None).
   - `__str__` returns `f"{node_id}.{output_slot}"` matching the existing string-ref convention (see `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` precedent and `vibecomfy/workflow.py:128-132` consumer that splits on `.`).
   - `__eq__` matches BOTH another Handle (by node_id+slot) AND its string form, so `handle == "12.0"` works without manual `str()`. Closes the silent type-erasure hole.
   - `__hash__` consistent with `__eq__`.

A2. Tighten `Handles` (plural wrapper at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11-35`) — change value type from `Mapping[str, Any]` to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. Update construction sites in `vibecomfy/blocks/` (notably `subgraph.py`) to wrap string refs as Handle instances. Backward-compat: callers passing raw strings must be coerced with a `DeprecationWarning` once per call site.

A3. `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs)` — typed authoring wrapper over `VibeWorkflow.add_node(...)` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122-126`). Returns an object with `.out(name_or_index) -> Handle` and `.id` (matches GraphBuilder's `id=` convention). Per the revised plan's API Concepts, this is the canonical authoring entry point. Does NOT promise mypy-grade safety; `T` parameterization deferred to a later phase that commits to overloads or codegen.

A4. `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract — implement the method signature, raise `NotImplementedError` with a clear message if `handle.output_type is None`. Documented now to prevent downstream code from forming against partial behavior before MP-6 ungates it.

A5. Lint/doctor coverage of `str(handle)` coercion — extend `vibecomfy doctor` (search `vibecomfy/cli/`) to flag:
   - Raw string refs in block code where a typed Handle is in scope (warning).
   - Sites where `str(handle)` flows into `connect()` or dict keys (warning).
   New rule name per plan §"Lint / Guardrail Ideas": `untyped_raw_ref`.

A6. Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates: build via `wf.node(...).out(...)`, assert `compile("api")` matches `compile("graphbuilder")` (modulo allowed-difference set: node IDs may move, MarkdownNote may be stripped). Use `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as one anchor.

### B. Custom-node lifecycle and lockfile enforcement (closes review §6, §7)

B1. `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` — new explicit verb. Refuses while a `run()` is in flight (see B2), closes existing `_comfy` context (mirror `stop()` at session.py:185-195 including the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow), reopens fresh `_comfy` (mirror `start()` at session.py:103-109), resets cached state carrying stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references. Implement equivalent for `ServerSession` (tear down and respawn the comfyui serve subprocess).

B2. **In-flight guard** on `session.stop()` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` — today `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (session.py:185-195) doesn't check if a `run()` is awaiting `queue_prompt_api`. Add a tracking field (e.g. `_inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None`) and have stop() either refuse with a clear `RuntimeError`, OR cancel-and-await. Audit current `session.stop()` call sites; if both options reasonable, expose `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`.

B3. **`ServerSession` external-mode handling** — when the session targets an external-managed `comfyui serve` (one VibeComfy didn't spawn), `reload_for_nodepack_change()` MUST NOT terminate it. Raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (new error class) with a clear message. Today no detection path or error class exists — both must be created.

B4. **`vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command** — write `custom_nodes.lock` after resolving any `semantic_label` values. Schema per plan §"Custom Nodes — Pinning, Hashes, and Lockfiles":
   - `git_commit_sha` (required primary pin — Git commit SHA-1, immutable).
   - `semantic_label` (optional advisory, e.g. "v1.4.0", "main").
   - `source_sha256` (optional per-node-class content digest, hex).
   Today's lockfile records `(name, sha, url)` only — see entries for `ComfyUI-KJNodes`, `ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. Extend format and migrate existing entries. Preserve `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename with backward-compat read.

B5. **`vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification verb** — read lockfile before `session.start()` (or as separate doctor invocation); verify each installed nodepack's git HEAD matches `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, verify the named class file's content hash. Default on mismatch: **FAIL CLOSED**. Provide `--allow-drift` opt-in to downgrade to warning.

B6. **Tests**: in-flight guard, reload verb (with fake nodepack), external-server detection (assert `ExternalServerRestartRequired` raised, external process untouched), lockfile round-trip, SHA verification (synthetic mismatch fails closed without `--allow-drift`, warns with it).

### C. RunPod smoke-test scaffold (new)

C1. Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or `tests/runpod/`, follow existing test layout). The test:
   - Uses the `runpod_lifecycle` Python package (already available in this workspace; check `pip show runpod_lifecycle` or look in the broader workspace for the package). The package provisions ephemeral RunPod GPU pods, runs work, and tears them down.
   - Provisions one pod (cheap GPU class — e.g. RTX 4090 or A40), installs vibecomfy on it, runs ONE representative ready template through the new `wf.node(...).out(...)` typed-Handle authoring path, submits via `EmbeddedSession.run(...)` after a `reload_for_nodepack_change()` call (to exercise B1 + B2 paths), asserts the run produces expected outputs, tears the pod down.
   - Marked `@pytest.mark.runpod` so it's OPT-IN — never runs in default `pytest` invocations or CI without explicit opt-in.
   - Documented in the relevant test README or `docs/`: how to run it (`pytest -m runpod`), what credentials are required (RunPod API key env var), expected wall-clock cost, and the safety contract that it tears down on success AND failure.

C2. **DO NOT EXECUTE the smoke test as part of this megaplan run.** Provisioning real GPU pods burns credentials and budget — the megaplan executor must not do this. The deliverable is the test code + docs; the user (or post-merge CI) runs it explicitly.

### CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS

- **Backward compatibility is non-negotiable.** All existing tests must pass unchanged. Existing blocks returning `Handles({"image": "12.0"})` must keep working; tightening must coerce-with-warning. Existing `custom_nodes.lock` must still load (handle `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` migration). Existing `session.stop()` callers must keep working.
- Do NOT implement the compile-time serialization gate (MP-3 territory).
- Do NOT implement schema validation, `/object_info` snapshot, or `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` (MP-6 territory).
- Do NOT implement VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, `wrap_api_dict`, multi-stage orchestration, or stage-aware run-dir naming / `flow_id` / `stage_index` in `RunResult` (MP-5 territory; SD-013 in the plan).
- Do NOT expand the block library beyond what's needed for parity tests (MP-3 territory).
- Hot-loading a nodepack into a running session WITHOUT reload is explicitly out of scope per the plan.
- The RunPod smoke test must NOT be invoked during the megaplan execute phase.

        Plan:

# Implementation Plan: P1 Foundation — Typed Handles, Custom-Node Lifecycle, RunPod Smoke Scaffold

## Overview

Goal: ship MP-2 (typed `Handle`) + MP-4 (custom-node lifecycle/lockfile enforcement) + a RunPod smoke-test scaffold as one squashable PR. Land the enforcement gates from the DSL plan and review without preempting MP-3 (block library / serialization gate), MP-5 (VibeFlow / `wrap_api_dict`), or MP-6 (schema-backed `output_type` / `run_until` ungating / named-output slot resolution).

Repo shape that matters:
- `Handles` (plural) at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11`; values are `Mapping[str, Any]`. `Handle` (singular) does not exist yet.
- `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:8` already imports `from vibecomfy.workflow import VibeWorkflow` — placing `Handle` in `vibecomfy/blocks/` would create a circular dep with `workflow.py` (FLAG-004 / correctness-2). Resolution: new neutral module `vibecomfy/handles.py`.
- `Handles({...})` construction sites span `subgraph.py:47`, `loaders.py`, `encoding.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `latent.py`, **`decode.py`**, **`video.py`** (scope-1 — full set confirmed by `Handles(` audit before coding).
- `VibeWorkflow.add_node` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122`) returns a mutable `VibeNode`. `connect` (`:128`) parses `from_ref.split(".", 1)`. `compile("api")` (`:187`) emits `[str(edge.from_node), int(edge.from_output)]` — **`int(edge.from_output)` rejects non-numeric output names** (FLAG-002 / correctness-1). Resolution: P1 `.out()` only accepts integer slots and digit strings; non-numeric names raise `NotImplementedError` with an explicit MP-6 reference.
- `connect`'s `to_ref` parameter is a target input path `"node.input_name"`, not an output reference (correctness-3). Only `from_ref` accepts a `Handle`.
- `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.connect` and `node_id` (`_utils.py:32-47`) accept `str | VibeNode` only; once blocks return `Handle` values these helpers must accept `Handle` too (callers-1).
- `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:185`) has no in-flight guard. `queue_prompt_api` await at `:144` is unshielded. `ServerSession.reconfigure` (`:281`) only reacts to argv changes.
- `ServerSession.process is None` is ambiguous between "external" and "owned-but-not-started/stopped" (FLAG-005 / issue_hints-3). Resolution: new explicit `_external: bool` field set by an `attach()` constructor / `external=True` param.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` currently does not re-export `EmbeddedSession`/`ServerSession` (all_locations-2). Resolution: export them plus `ExternalServerRestartRequired` + `SessionConfig`.
- `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` is a single positional-`path` parser (all_locations-3). Resolution: add `--lint` and `--allow-drift` flags directly to the existing parser; do **not** add a `lockfile` subcommand (drift verification is integrated into the normal flow).
- `custom_nodes.lock` is a 2-line `<name> <git_sha> <url>` whitespace file. New format: TOML (`tomllib`-readable; legacy lines remain readable as a migration shim).
- `pyproject.toml:33-34` has `asyncio_mode = "auto"` and **no markers / no collection hooks**. Marker registration alone does not gate execution (FLAG-003 / issue_hints-2 / all_locations-1). Resolution: `tests/conftest.py` adds `--runpod` option and a `pytest_collection_modifyitems` hook that deselects `runpod`-marked items unless that option is passed.
- `runpod_lifecycle` is a sibling repo loaded via the path/env logic at `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py:10-17`.
- Parity reference: `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` (uses private `node()` helper, raw string `connect()` — proves native builders work but is **not** the target API shape). Two more anchor templates picked from `ready_templates/image/` and `ready_templates/video/` to give the parity proof real corpus weight (FLAG-001 / issue_hints-1).
- `docs/authoring.md` currently teaches the deprecated raw-string `Handles(text=node.id)` shape (scope-2). Resolution: update authoring docs in this PR.

Constraints (hard, unchanged):
- Backward compatibility: every existing test passes unchanged. `Handle.__eq__` accepts the bare-node-id form when `output_slot == 0` to keep `tests/test_blocks.py:41` green (settled SD-1).
- Old lockfile schema (`name sha url`) loads via migration shim. `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename works on read.
- No mypy-grade typing claim (P1 = typed metadata + lint).
- No serialization gate (MP-3), no schema/`output_type` integration (MP-6), no VibeFlow/`wrap_api_dict` (MP-5).
- Smoke test is scaffolded only — never executed by the megaplan run.

## Phase A: Typed Handle Scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

### Step 1: Create `Handle` in a neutral module (`vibecomfy/handles.py` — new)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/handles.py` (top-level, no dependencies on `vibecomfy.workflow` or `vibecomfy.blocks`):
   ```python
   from __future__ import annotations
   from dataclasses import dataclass

   @dataclass(frozen=True)
   class Handle:
       node_id: str
       output_slot: int | str = 0   # int or digit-string only in P1
       output_type: str | None = None   # populated by MP-6
       name: str | None = None      # human-readable label; not used for slot resolution

       def __str__(self) -> str:
           return f"{self.node_id}.{self.output_slot}"

       def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
           if isinstance(other, Handle):
               return self.node_id == other.node_id and str(self.output_slot) == str(other.output_slot)
           if isinstance(other, str):
               canonical = f"{self.node_id}.{self.output_slot}"
               if other == canonical:
                   return True
               # Backcompat: bare node-id matches when slot == 0 (keeps tests/test_blocks.py:41 green)
               if str(self.output_slot) == "0" and other == self.node_id:
                   return True
               return False
           return NotImplemented

       def __hash__(self) -> int:
           return hash((self.node_id, str(self.output_slot)))
   ```
2. **Re-export** `Handle` from `vibecomfy/__init__.py` (`from vibecomfy.handles import Handle`) so `from vibecomfy import Handle` is the canonical public path.
3. **Re-export** `Handle` from `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` for backwards-friendly access from block authors.

### Step 2: Tighten `Handles` plural wrapper with coercion warning (`vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `Handles.__init__` to coerce raw strings to `Handle`:
   - `"node.slot"` → `Handle(node_id, output_slot=slot)` (split on first `.`; preserve raw token, do not cast to int — we accept digit strings).
   - bare `"id"` → `Handle(id, 0)`.
   - Each coercion emits `warnings.warn(..., DeprecationWarning, stacklevel=2)` exactly once per source location, keyed on `(filename, lineno)` from `inspect.stack()[1]`.
2. **Update** the `values` field annotation to `Mapping[str, Handle]` (documentation; no runtime enforcement beyond coercion).

### Step 3: Audit and migrate ALL block construction sites (`vibecomfy/blocks/{subgraph,loaders,encoding,sampling,save,latent,decode,video}.py` — full sweep, plus `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Audit** with `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` to enumerate every construction site before editing — confirms the list (subgraph, loaders, encoding, sampling, save, latent, decode, video) and surfaces any other module not enumerated above.
2. **Replace** every `Handles({"x": "<id>"})` and `Handles({"x": f"{node.id}.{slot}"})` with `Handles({"x": Handle(node_id=..., output_slot=..., name="x")})`. Where blocks were storing bare node IDs (e.g. `Handles(image=node.id)`), the canonical replacement is `Handle(node_id=node.id, output_slot=0, name="image")`. The bare-form `__eq__` extension keeps existing tests green.
3. **Update** `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:32-47` so `node_id()` and `connect()` accept `Handle` in addition to `str | VibeNode`:
   - `node_id(value)` → if `Handle`, return `value.node_id`.
   - `connect(workflow, source, ...)` → if `source` is a `Handle`, route through `workflow.connect(str(source), ...)` instead of constructing a manual `node.slot` string. Drop the `output_slot` kwarg precedence when source is a `Handle` (the slot is in the Handle).

### Step 4: Add `wf.node(...)` typed authoring wrapper (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Import** `Handle` lazily at top of `workflow.py` (`from vibecomfy.handles import Handle` — no circular dep since `handles.py` has no other vibecomfy imports).
2. **Add** internal class `_NodeBuilder` (private, returned from `VibeWorkflow.node`) and the new public method:
   ```python
   def node(self, class_type: str, **kwargs: Any) -> "_NodeBuilder":
       node = self.add_node(class_type)
       for key, value in kwargs.items():
           if isinstance(value, Handle):
               self.connect(value, f"{node.id}.{key}")
           else:
               node.inputs[key] = value
       return _NodeBuilder(workflow=self, node=node)
   ```
   `_NodeBuilder` exposes:
   - `id: str` (mirrors GraphBuilder's `id=` convention).
   - `out(slot: int | str) -> Handle` — accepts integers and digit strings only. Non-numeric strings raise:
     ```python
     raise NotImplementedError(
         f"Named outputs (e.g. .out({slot!r})) require MP-6 schema integration; "
         "pass an integer slot for P1."
     )
     ```
     Implementation guard: `int(str(slot))` inside a try/except — raise on `ValueError`. `output_type` stays `None` (MP-6 populates).
3. **Extend** `VibeWorkflow.connect`'s `from_ref` parameter typing only. Final signature:
   ```python
   def connect(self, from_ref: str | Handle, to_ref: str) -> "VibeWorkflow":
       ...
   ```
   `to_ref` stays `str` — it is a target input path `"node.input_name"`, not an output handle. Coerce `Handle` via `str(handle)` before splitting.
4. **Defer** `T` parameterization — `Handle` is intentionally non-generic for P1.

### Step 5: Implement `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `def run_until(self, handle: Handle) -> Any:` that raises:
   - `NotImplementedError("wf.run_until is gated on MP-6 schema integration; handle.output_type is None. Manually attach SaveImage/PreviewImage/SaveAudio.")` when `handle.output_type is None`.
   - A second `NotImplementedError("wf.run_until runner is not implemented yet; gated on MP-6.")` when `output_type` is hand-populated (so neither caller path forms against partial behavior).

### Step 6: Doctor lint rule `untyped_raw_ref` (`vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Migrate** the doctor argparse without breaking `vibecomfy doctor PATH` callers:
   - Keep `path` as the single positional argument.
   - Add `--lint` (`action="store_true"`, default False) and `--allow-drift` (`action="store_true"`, default False) flags on the existing parser. No subcommand group, no breakage of `tests/test_cli.py`.
2. **When `args.lint` is set**, run an AST-walker over the loaded `.py` builder source (resolve via `_json_path_for_reference`'s sibling logic; skip JSON refs):
   - **Source-side flag:** a string literal matching `r"^\w+\.\w+$"` passed positionally to `workflow.connect(...)` when a `Handle`-returning call (`wf.node(...).out(...)`) appears earlier in the same function.
   - **Coercion-side flag:** a call expression `str(<name>)` where `<name>` was bound from `... .out(...)` in the same function (single-assignment heuristic).
3. **Emit** warnings via stdout prefixed `- untyped_raw_ref:` and exit code 0 (warning-only). When `--lint` is unset, doctor behavior is exactly as today.

### Step 7: Real-ready-template parity tests (`tests/parity/test_p1_typed_handle_parity.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Pick** three actual ready templates as parity anchors. Required: `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py`. Two more chosen from existing builders by structural variety (one image template using a different model family — e.g. `ready_templates/image/z_image.py` if it has a sufficiently small graph, or another image builder; one video template if a small enough graph exists, otherwise a second image variant).
2. **For each anchor**, write a sibling fixture (e.g. `tests/parity/fixtures/flux2_klein_4b_typed.py`) that reproduces the *same* graph (same class types, same model assets, same prompts, same widget values, same sink) using only `wf.node(...).out(<int_slot>)` and Handle-aware `wf.connect(handle, "node.input")`. No private `node()` helper, no raw string source refs.
3. **Assert:**
   - **Self-parity (must pass without GraphBuilder installed):** for the typed-handle build, normalize node-ID ordering and strip `MarkdownNote` nodes, then assert the structural shape (class types per node, sorted edge tuples, widget values) matches what compile produces. This is the always-on parity smoke.
   - **GraphBuilder parity (skipped when `comfy_execution` is missing, like `test_workflow_core.py:67`):** `wf.compile("api") == wf.compile("graphbuilder")` for the typed build, modulo node-ID ordering and `MarkdownNote` stripping.
   - **Corpus parity (must pass):** the typed-handle build's `compile("api")` matches the **original ready template's** `compile("api")` modulo the same allowed-difference set (node-ID ordering, `MarkdownNote` stripping). This proves the typed-handle rewrite actually mirrors the corpus, not a synthetic graph.
4. **Do not** rewrite the original `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` in place; that's MP-5 territory. The typed-handle versions live under `tests/parity/fixtures/`.

### Step 8: Handle/wrapper unit tests (`tests/test_handle.py` — new)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cover:**
   - `Handle.__str__`, `__eq__` against another Handle, against `"node.slot"`, against bare `"node"` (slot=0 only — explicit assertion).
   - `__hash__` consistency for `("12", 0)` vs `("12", "0")`.
   - `Handles` coercion-with-DeprecationWarning (use `pytest.warns`) — same call site twice → exactly one warning.
   - `from vibecomfy import Handle` import path.
   - `wf.node(...).out(0)` returns Handle that compiles cleanly through `compile("api")`.
   - `wf.node(...).out("CONDITIONING")` raises `NotImplementedError` referencing MP-6.
   - `wf.run_until(handle)` raises `NotImplementedError` whenever `handle.output_type is None`.
   - `wf.connect(handle, "node.input_name")` succeeds; `wf.connect("a.0", handle)` (Handle in to_ref position) is **rejected at type-check time** (assert with `mypy --strict` skipped; for runtime, document only — no defensive isinstance check needed since `to_ref: str` typing is the contract).

### Step 9: Update authoring docs (`docs/authoring.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** every example showing `Handles(text=node.id)` or `Handles({"image": "12.0"})` with the canonical `Handles({"image": Handle(node_id=node.id, output_slot=0, name="image")})` form. Keep the bare-form coercion noted as a deprecated path with one migration example.
2. **Add** a one-paragraph "Typed Handles in P1" subsection explaining: P1 ships typed metadata + `wf.node(...).out(int_slot)`; named-output strings raise `NotImplementedError` until MP-6.

## Phase B: Custom-Node Lifecycle and Lockfile (closes review §6, §7)

### Step 10: In-flight task tracking on sessions (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `self._inflight_run: asyncio.Task | None = None` to both `EmbeddedSession.__init__` and `ServerSession.__init__`.
2. **Wrap** the entire body of `run()` (not just `queue_prompt_api`) so the in-flight tracker captures the full critical section — queue submit + watchdog finalization + metadata write. Implementation: at the top of `run()`, set `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()`; in `finally`, set it back to `None`. This addresses callers-2 (watchdog/metadata work after the inner queue future).
3. **Modify** `EmbeddedSession.stop` and `ServerSession.stop` to take `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`:
   - `_inflight_run` set + `wait_for_inflight=True`: `await self._inflight_run` (don't cancel — HiddenSwitch can't be aborted mid-prompt per SD-013(a)). Catch and re-raise any exception from the awaited task before tearing down so callers see the run's own failure.
   - `_inflight_run` set + `wait_for_inflight=False`: raise `RuntimeError("session.stop() called while a run is in flight; pass wait_for_inflight=True or call after run completes")`.
   - No `_inflight_run`: existing behavior unchanged → all callers in `tests/test_runtime_session.py` keep passing.

### Step 11: `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `async def reload_for_nodepack_change(self, *, reason: str) -> None` to `EmbeddedSession`. Strict-refuse if a run is in flight (no `wait_for_inflight` toggle on the reload verb itself per assumptions):
   - If `_inflight_run` set and not done: raise `RuntimeError("reload_for_nodepack_change refused: run in flight")`.
   - Mirror `stop()`'s `_context.__aexit__` block (preserve the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow at `:190-192`).
   - Reset `self._comfy = None`, `self._context = None`, `self._schema_provider = None`, `self._schema_warning_emitted = False`, `self.last_fingerprint = None`.
   - Call `await self.start()` to reopen a fresh `Comfy()` context.
   - `logger.info("reload_for_nodepack_change: %s", reason)`.

### Step 12: `ServerSession` ownership flag and reload (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `_external: bool` field to `ServerSession.__init__`, defaulting to `False`. Add a classmethod / constructor `ServerSession.attach(url: str, *, config: SessionConfig | None = None) -> "ServerSession"` that builds an instance with `_external=True`, `process=None`, and the provided URL — explicit external-mode entry point. (FLAG-005 / issue_hints-3 fix: external mode is now an explicit field, not a `process is None` guess.)
2. **Add** `async def reload_for_nodepack_change(self, *, reason: str) -> None` to `ServerSession`:
   - If `self._external is True`: raise new `ExternalServerRestartRequired(f"vibecomfy does not own this server (url={self.url}); restart externally and reconnect to apply nodepack change ({reason})")`. **Do not** mutate `self.process` or `self.url`.
   - If `self._external is False`: enforce in-flight guard (raise `RuntimeError` if `_inflight_run` is set), then `await self.stop()` followed by `await self.start()`.

### Step 13: `ExternalServerRestartRequired` exception + runtime exports (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`, `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Define** `class ExternalServerRestartRequired(RuntimeError): """Raised when reload_for_nodepack_change cannot proceed against an externally-managed comfyui serve."""` near the top of `session.py`.
2. **Update** `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` to export the lifecycle surface acceptance requires: `EmbeddedSession`, `ServerSession`, `SessionConfig`, `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (and `RunResult` if not already exported). This satisfies the acceptance "exposed in `vibecomfy.runtime`" line.

### Step 14: Lockfile schema migration + reader/writer (`vibecomfy/node_packs/lockfile.py` — new module)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/node_packs/lockfile.py`:
   - `@dataclass class LockEntry: name: str; git_commit_sha: str; url: str; semantic_label: str | None = None; source_sha256: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)` (`source_sha256` maps class-file relative path → hex digest).
   - `def read_lockfile(path: Path = Path("custom_nodes.lock")) -> list[LockEntry]`: detect format. Legacy lines (3 whitespace tokens, no `[section]` or `=` syntax) → `LockEntry(name, git_commit_sha=token2, url=token3)`. New format (TOML, detected by leading `[` or `=`) → parse via `tomllib`. Accept `git_sha` and `git_commit_sha` synonyms when reading TOML.
   - `def write_lockfile(entries: list[LockEntry], path: Path) -> None` — always writes TOML keyed by `name` (one `[<name>]` table per entry).
2. **No edits to the existing `custom_nodes.lock` in this PR** — the file rewrites itself the first time `vibecomfy nodes lock` runs after this lands, preserving review-friendly diff scope.

### Step 15: `vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command (`vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Register** new subcommand `lock` with `_cmd_nodes_lock(args)` that:
   - Reads the existing lockfile (legacy or TOML) via `read_lockfile`.
   - For each entry, resolves `semantic_label` to a `git_commit_sha` if a checkout is present (run `git -C <pack_dir> rev-parse HEAD`); if no checkout, keeps existing `git_commit_sha` and leaves `semantic_label` advisory.
   - Optional `--with-source-sha256` flag (default off) computes per-class-file `*.py` SHA-256 digests for installed packs.
   - Writes new TOML via `write_lockfile`.
2. **Update** `register(subparsers)` at `vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py:101` to add the new subparser.

### Step 16: `vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification (`vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** verification pass to `_cmd_doctor`, gated by `--allow-drift`:
   - Read lockfile via `read_lockfile`.
   - For each entry, locate the installed nodepack (search `vendor/<name>` first, then any other path that `vibecomfy.node_packs` consumers use today — confirm during implementation by reading `vibecomfy/node_packs/`).
   - Run `git -C <pack_dir> rev-parse HEAD` (subprocess) and compare to `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` is present, hash the corresponding files and compare.
2. **Mismatch policy:**
   - Default fail-closed → exit `1` with `Layer: nodepack lockfile drift` and a per-pack diff.
   - `--allow-drift` → emit warnings, exit `0`.
3. **Wiring:** runs *before* the existing schema-validation pass so a drift error short-circuits with a clear message.

### Step 17: Lifecycle, lockfile, and doctor tests (`tests/test_runtime_session.py` extensions, `tests/test_nodes_lock.py` — new, `tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **In-flight guard** (extend `tests/test_runtime_session.py`): use the existing `FakeComfy` fixture; arrange `queue_prompt_api` to block on an `asyncio.Event`; assert `stop(wait_for_inflight=False)` raises `RuntimeError`; assert `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` waits cleanly. Also assert that exceptions raised by the in-flight `run()` task propagate through `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` before context teardown.
2. **Reload verb (embedded):** assert `reload_for_nodepack_change(reason="test")` calls `__aexit__` then `__aenter__` once each; that `_comfy` is a fresh instance; that the in-flight refusal triggers when a run is pending.
3. **External-server detection:** instantiate via `ServerSession.attach(url="http://127.0.0.1:8188")`; assert `_external is True`; assert `reload_for_nodepack_change` raises `ExternalServerRestartRequired`; assert `self.process` and `self.url` are unchanged. Counter-test: `ServerSession()` (default constructor) with `_external=False` and `process=None` (not yet started) → reload calls `stop()` then `start()` (mock both).
4. **Lockfile round-trip** (`tests/test_nodes_lock.py`): write a legacy 3-token line, read it, write back as TOML, read TOML, assert parity. Confirm `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename works on TOML read.
5. **SHA verification** (`tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py`): create a fake pack dir; monkeypatch the `git rev-parse HEAD` subprocess call. Three cases: (a) match → exit 0 / no mismatch output; (b) mismatch without `--allow-drift` → exit 1; (c) mismatch with `--allow-drift` → warning, exit 0.

## Phase C: RunPod Smoke-Test Scaffold (real opt-in gate)

### Step 18: Pytest opt-in gate (`tests/conftest.py` — new or extended, `pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add `tests/conftest.py`** with a real opt-in gate (FLAG-003 / issue_hints-2 / all_locations-1 — marker registration alone is insufficient):
   ```python
   import pytest

   def pytest_addoption(parser):
       parser.addoption(
           "--runpod",
           action="store_true",
           default=False,
           help="Run opt-in RunPod GPU smoke tests (provisions real pods; requires RUNPOD_API_KEY).",
       )

   def pytest_collection_modifyitems(config, items):
       if config.getoption("--runpod"):
           return  # explicit opt-in: collect everything
       skip_runpod = pytest.mark.skip(reason="opt-in: pass --runpod to enable")
       for item in items:
           if "runpod" in item.keywords:
               item.add_marker(skip_runpod)
   ```
2. **Register the marker** in `pyproject.toml` under `[tool.pytest.ini_options]`:
   ```toml
   markers = [
     "runpod: opt-in smoke tests that provision real RunPod GPU pods (requires --runpod and RUNPOD_API_KEY).",
   ]
   ```
   This pairs with the conftest hook so `pytest -m runpod` shows test IDs but `pytest` (no flags) and `pytest -m runpod` *without* `--runpod` both skip them. Default `pytest` with `RUNPOD_API_KEY` set in the environment also still skips — the gate is `--runpod` flag, not credential presence.

### Step 19: Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `tests/smoke/__init__.py` (empty) and `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py`.
2. **Test body**:
   - At module top: `pytestmark = pytest.mark.runpod`.
   - At test top: skip if `os.environ.get("RUNPOD_API_KEY")` is unset (graceful skip even when `--runpod` was passed without creds).
   - Import `runpod_lifecycle` (handle `ImportError` → `pytest.skip` referencing `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT`).
   - Provision one cheap pod (RTX 4090 or A40) using `runpod_lifecycle`'s API. Wrap in `try/finally` so teardown runs on success **and** failure.
   - On the pod: install vibecomfy from current branch, build a 3-node typed-Handle workflow via `wf.node(...).out(<int_slot>)` (mirrors a parity fixture from Step 7), call `await session.reload_for_nodepack_change(reason="smoke")`, then `await session.run(wf)`.
   - Assert at least one output path exists in `result.outputs`.
   - Tear down pod in `finally`.
3. **Do not call this test in this megaplan run.** Acceptance verifies file existence, marker, and skip behavior — not execution.

### Step 20: Document the smoke contract (`docs/runpod_smoke.md` — new, plus pointer in `docs/custom_nodes.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Document**: how to run (`pytest --runpod -m runpod tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py`), required env (`RUNPOD_API_KEY`, optional `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT`), expected wall-clock + cost (~5–10 min, ~$0.05–$0.20), and the safety contract: teardown runs on success and failure, default `pytest` invocations never collect this test (the conftest `--runpod` gate is the line of defense, marker registration alone is not).

## Execution Order

1. Phase A first (Handle module + tightened `Handles` + `wf.node().out()` + full block migration sweep + parity tests against real ready templates + authoring-docs update + lint flag). Lands the typed-Handle surface. Unblocks Phase B fixtures.
2. Phase B second (in-flight tracking → reload verb → ServerSession `_external` flag → exception + runtime exports → lockfile module → CLI lock subcommand → doctor verification → all lifecycle/lockfile tests). Pure backend, no test cross-contamination with Phase A.
3. Phase C last (conftest opt-in gate → marker registration → smoke file → docs). Pure additive.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_handle.py` — fastest signal on Handle correctness.
2. `pytest tests/test_blocks.py tests/test_workflow_core.py` — Handle migration didn't regress block authoring or compile.
3. `pytest tests/parity/` — typed-handle parity against real ready templates.
4. `pytest tests/test_runtime_session.py tests/test_nodes_lock.py tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py` — Phase B lifecycle + lockfile.
5. `pytest` (full suite, no flags) — verifies every existing test passes unchanged AND that runpod tests are deselected.
6. `pytest --collect-only -m runpod` — should list one test (`tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py::...`); `pytest -m runpod` without `--runpod` should report it as skipped, not executed.
7. Manual sanity check: `vibecomfy doctor --lint <typed-handle parity fixture>` should print at least one `untyped_raw_ref` warning when given a deliberately-bad fixture; `vibecomfy nodes lock` should rewrite the lockfile in TOML and `vibecomfy doctor <ready_template>` should fail-closed when the on-disk pack SHA is mutated.

  "assumptions": [
    "`Handle` lives at `vibecomfy/handles.py` (top-level neutral module). Both `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` and `vibecomfy/workflow.py` import from there. Re-exported from `vibecomfy.__init__` and `vibecomfy.blocks` for ergonomics.",
    "P1 `.out()` accepts int and digit-string slots only. Non-numeric names raise NotImplementedError referencing MP-6. The Desired-Authoring-Experience example using `.out('CONDITIONING')` is aspirational for MP-6 \u2014 P1 examples in `docs/authoring.md` use `.out(0)` etc.",
    "`ServerSession` external mode is set via `ServerSession.attach(url=..., config=...)` classmethod or `_external=True` constructor param. Default constructor (today's behavior) sets `_external=False`. `process is None` is no longer used as a detection signal.",
    "RunPod opt-in gate is `--runpod` CLI flag enforced in `tests/conftest.py` via `pytest_collection_modifyitems`. Marker registration in `pyproject.toml` is supporting infrastructure for the hook, not the gate itself. Default `pytest` (with or without `RUNPOD_API_KEY`) does not collect or execute the smoke test.",
    "`Handle.__eq__` accepts the bare-node-id string form when `output_slot == 0` to keep `tests/test_blocks.py:41` passing \u2014 settled SD-1 from prior iteration debt.",
    "Lockfile new format is TOML (`tomllib`-readable); legacy whitespace-triple format remains readable for migration via auto-detect. The existing `custom_nodes.lock` file is not touched in this PR \u2014 it is rewritten the next time `vibecomfy nodes lock` runs.",
    "Doctor argparse stays single-positional with new `--lint` and `--allow-drift` boolean flags. No subcommand group; `vibecomfy doctor PATH` callers continue to work unchanged.",
    "Parity-test corpus anchors: `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` is required; the other two are picked during implementation from `ready_templates/image/` and `ready_templates/video/` based on graph simplicity (small enough to write a typed-handle mirror cleanly).",
    "Block-migration sweep uses `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` as the authoritative enumeration step before any edits, so `decode.py` / `video.py` and any other unenumerated module are caught.",
    "`run()` in-flight tracking uses `asyncio.current_task()` set at the top of `run()` (not the inner queue future), so `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` awaits the full critical section including watchdog finalization and metadata write.",
    "Flux 4B builder is NOT rewritten in place; the typed-handle mirror lives under `tests/parity/fixtures/`. In-place rewrite is MP-5 territory."

    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"

    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: Checked B3 against the current `ServerSession` shape in `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`.     "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"

The plan proposes `vibecomfy doctor --lint <path>` and possibly `vibecomfy doctor lockfile <workflow_path>`, but `doctor` is currently a single parser with one positional `path`, not a subcommand group. This is implementable, but the plan does not spell out the parser migration needed to add `--lint`, `--allow-drift`, and a potential `lockfile` subcommand without breaking existing `vibecomfy doctor PATH` invocations.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers-1",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: Checked callers of `workflow.connect` and the block helper `vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect`. Existing block callers pass bare node ids, dotted strings, and `VibeNode` instances; after the plan migrates block return values to `Handle`, those handles will commonly flow through `_utils.connect`, which currently accepts only `str | VibeNode | None`. The plan updates `VibeWorkflow.connect`, but it must also update `_utils.node_id`/`connect` typing and behavior deliberately or the main block-helper caller path remains an implicit `str(handle)` coercion path.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers-2",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: Checked callers of `ServerSession.stop` in tests and command code. Existing code calls `await session.stop()` with no arguments, so adding `wait_for_inflight: bool = True` is backwards compatible at the call-site level. The risk is semantic rather than syntactic: if stop waits only for the queued prompt future, as the plan says, it may close the process/context before the rest of `run()` finishes watchdog finalization and metadata writes.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  }
]
